# parser (libs/net/src/http.zig of the Zig repository) over 3,821 requests: the corpus of
# valid and smuggling requests, every truncation of three of them, and seeded random
# mutations. One vector per line: the escaped request, TAB, the verdict. Escapes are \r,
# \n, \t and \xHH; other bytes are literal. A verdict is "err" TAB the error name, or
# "ok" TAB method TAB path TAB query TAB version TAB header count TAB chunked (0 or 1)
# TAB body length TAB the 64-bit FNV-1a of every header as name ":" value "\n", in hex.
